Use the repository manifest and chat-pair manifest as authoritative mapping tables. Output a complete directory tree, write every file, seed benchmark and gold data directories, create environment-based bootstrap scripts, and leave explicit TODO markers only where the conversation never supplied final content.EOFcat > prompts/ai_handoff_one_paragraph.md <<'EOF'Rebuild the entire UAKS repository locally from the supplied conversation artifacts by externalizing every drafted specification into its own file, preserving all IDs, ontology boundaries, trust states, relation types, manifests, and lifecycle rules, creating placeholders for any still-undrafted documents, generating the full directory structure, bootstrap scripts, seed data folders, and implementation scaffolding, and ensuring nothing from the architecture, governance, refinery, retrieval, assembly, portal, operations, evaluation, integrity, or launch layers is lost, flattened, or silently altered.EOFcat > scripts/bootstrap_env.sh <<EOF#!/usr/bin/env bashexport PROJECT_SLUG="${PROJECT_SLUG}"export REPO_ROOT="${REPO_ROOT}"export AUTHOR_NAME="${AUTHOR_NAME}"export DATE_STAMP="${DATE_STAMP}"export DEFAULT_BRANCH="${DEFAULT_BRANCH}"export PYTHON_BIN="${PYTHON_BIN}"EOFchmod +x scripts/bootstrap_env.shcat > scripts/print_tree.py <<'EOF'from pathlib import Pathdef walk(path: Path, prefix: str = ""):    entries = sorted([p for p in path.iterdir()], key=lambda p: (p.is_file(), p.name))    for i, entry in enumerate(entries):        connector = "└── " if i == len(entries) - 1 else "├── "        print(prefix + connector + entry.name)        if entry.is_dir():            extension = "    " if i == len(entries) - 1 else "│   "            walk(entry, prefix + extension)root = Path(".")print(root.resolve().name)walk(root)EOFif [ ! -d .git ]; then  git init -b "${DEFAULT_BRANCH}"fiecho "Repository scaffold created at: ${REPO_ROOT}"echo "Next step: paste drafted document content into the generated markdown files."
```

export PROJECT_SLUG="uaks"export REPO_ROOT="$HOME/work/uaks"export AUTHOR_NAME="AJP"bash scripts/bootstrap_uaks_repo.sh
```

## 5. AI Handoff Prompt

### Full version

Use this when handing the job to an AI engineer or coding agent.

Plain text

```
You are the repository-assembly and implementation engineer for the Unified Atomic Knowledge Substrate (UAKS). Your job is to reconstruct the entire local repository from the architecture conversation artifact set without losing semantics, document IDs, ontology boundaries, trust states, relation vocabulary, provenance rules, or lifecycle logic. Create the full repository tree, externalize every drafted specification into its own markdown file, preserve all canonical IDs and titles, create manifests for artifacts and chat-pair lineage, add placeholders only for content that was never fully drafted, and scaffold implementation directories for source intake, text refinery, code refinery, analysis normalization, graph storage, vector retrieval, content-addressable storage, GraphQL, assembly, portal projection, operations, evaluation, integrity verification, and launch control. Preserve the distinction between source evidence, primary atoms, and derivative analytical atoms. Preserve supersession, contradiction, provenance, and trust policies exactly. Generate bootstrap scripts, sample data directories, benchmark and gold fixture directories, and implementation TODO markers where code does not yet exist. Do not compress or merge distinct artifact families. Do not silently rename canonical documents. Produce a final repository tree, a completeness report, a list of unresolved placeholders, and a verification pass confirming that nothing from the conversation was dropped or flattened.
```

## 7. Best next move

Run the bootstrap script first, then paste the drafted documents into the generated files in repository order: governance, architecture, schemas, refinery, retrieval/assembly, interface/portal, operations, evaluation, integrity, launch.
